const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

function dice_main()
    (x646_res, x649__goal, x650_res,) = chunk_1()
    (x1045__goal, x1048_res, x1049__goal,) = chunk_2(x646_res, x649__goal, x650_res)
    (x1444_res, x1447__goal, x1448_res,) = chunk_3(x1045__goal, x1048_res, x1049__goal)
    (x1843__goal, x1846_res, x1847__goal,) = chunk_4(x1444_res, x1447__goal, x1448_res)
    (x2242_res, x2245__goal, x2246_res,) = chunk_5(x1843__goal, x1846_res, x1847__goal)
    (x2641__goal, x2644_res, x2645__goal,) = chunk_6(x2242_res, x2245__goal, x2246_res)
    (x3040_res, x3043__goal, x3044_res,) = chunk_7(x2641__goal, x2644_res, x2645__goal)
    (x3439__goal, x3442_res, x3443__goal,) = chunk_8(x3040_res, x3043__goal, x3044_res)
    (x3838_res, x3841__goal, x3842_res,) = chunk_9(x3439__goal, x3442_res, x3443__goal)
    (x4237__goal, x4240_res, x4241__goal,) = chunk_10(x3838_res, x3841__goal, x3842_res)
    x4452_result = chunk_11(x4237__goal, x4240_res, x4241__goal)
    return x4452_result
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
